### Assistant

_source line 84_

GSM8K 数据集缓存在 `/root/.cache/huggingface/` 但 `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1` 下 `datasets` 库可能还是尝试联网验证。问题是 `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1` 只对 `transformers` 有效，`datasets` 需要 `HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE=1`。

用两个离线变量重新提交。

Same error - `HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE=1` still doesn't make `datasets` use the cached version. The datasets library requires `HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE=1` but it also needs the dataset to be in its processed cache format, not just the hub cache.

Let me check what's in the cache:
- `/root/.cache/huggingface/hub/datasets--openai--gsm8k/` exists (hub cache)
- But `datasets` library also has its own cache at `~/.cache/huggingface/datasets/`

The issue might be that the dataset was never fully downloaded/processed through the datasets library, or the cache path is different.
 
The error `OfflineModeIsEnabled` confirms that `HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE=1` is being recognized, but the dataset isn't cached in the format the library expects. The `dataset_module_factory` can't resolve the dataset in offline mode because it's not finding it in the right cache location. I should try removing `HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE=1` and keeping only `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1` for the model instead.

Actually, wait—the Director mentioned the model is already local, so `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1` is meant to prevent model-related Hub calls. The dataset download itself should be fine since it's small and quick. But newer versions of `datasets` use `huggingface_hub` under the hood, so `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1` might affect dataset operations too.

The real issue is that setting `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1` globally blocks all Hub access, including dataset downloads, which fails because the dataset cache isn't properly formatted. I need to either pre-download the dataset separately before running in offline mode, or skip offline mode entirely if the network issue was just transient.

Actually, `TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1` might be a better option since it only affects the transformers library and would let the datasets library still download what it needs. Let me explore whether that's the right approach here.

Looking at the eval_gsm8k.py source to see if I can pass a local dataset path instead.

```text
Same error again! `TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1` is also triggering the offline mode for datasets. This is because newer versions of the `huggingface_hub` library set `HF_HUB_OFFLINE` when `TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1` is set, which then affects datasets as well.

Let me check the eval_gsm8k.py source to understand how it loads the dataset and model, and whether I can modify the approach.

Actually, the real problem is that the network on the server is having issues. The Director's intent was to prevent network calls for the model (which is local), but the dataset needs network access.


I'm considering two options: either remove the offline flags entirely and hope the network is stable now, or check if eval_gsm8k.py has a way to load the dataset from a local cache. The core issue is that `TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE` cascades to `HF_HUB_OFFLINE` in the newer HuggingFace ecosystem, blocking all network access when I only need to avoid it for the model.
```

The solution is to use `HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE=1` but NOT `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1`. Actually wait - `HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE=1` caused the same error because the datasets library checks `HF_HUB_OFFLINE` too.
 
Looking at the cache structure, the dataset is there at the expected path, but when I try to load it with `load_dataset("openai/gsm8k", "main", split="test")`, the library attempts to resolve the dataset from the Hub first. In offline mode, it should fall back to the cached version, but instead I'm hitting an `OfflineModeIsEnabled` error - which suggests the datasets library version might be too old to handle offline fallback properly.

I could work around this by loading directly from the cached path, enabling a network proxy if available, modifying the script to accept a local path parameter, or adjusting the offline mode settings. Since this is westb-23072 and not AutoDL, I should check if there's network connectivity or a proxy available. The quickest solution would be to modify the eval script to load from the local cache directory directly.

I'm thinking about how the datasets library handles offline mode—it seems to raise an error rather than gracefully falling back to cached data. I could try wrapping the load call in error handling to attempt offline loading first, then fall back to online if that fails, or use `load_from_disk` to read the cached dataset directly.

Let me examine what's actually stored in the cache directory to understand the format better.

      "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nGSM8K Test Set Evaluation.\\n\\nEvaluates model accuracy on GSM8K test split by generating reasoning traces\\nand comparing extracted numeric answers against gold answers.\\n\\nSupports greedy decoding and majority voting (self-consistency).\\n\\nInput:  model path\\nOutput: accuracy printed to stdout + saved to results JSON\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport math\\nimport logging\\nimport os\\nimport random\\nimport re\\nfrom collections import Counter\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport torch\\nfrom datasets import load_dataset\\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer\\nfrom model_utils import load_pruned_model\\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\\n\\nlogging.basicConfig(\\n    level=logging.INFO,\\n    format=\\\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\\\",\\n)\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\n\\ndef set_seed(seed: int):\\n    random.seed(seed)\\n    np.random.seed(seed)\\n    torch.manual_seed(seed)\\n    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)\\n\\n\\ndef _extract_boxed(text: str) -> str | None:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract content from \\\\\\\\boxed{...} with balanced brace matching.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    idx = text.rfind(\\\"\\\\\\\\boxed{\\\")\\n    if idx == -1:\\n        return None\\n    depth = 0\\n    start = idx + len(\\\"\\\\\\\\boxed{\\\")\\n    for i in range(start, len(text)):\\n        if text[i] == \\\"{\\\":\\n            depth += 1\\n        elif text[i] == \\\"}\\\":\\n            if depth == 0:\\n                return text[start:i]\\n            depth -= 1\\n    return None\\n\\n\\ndef _clean_latex_number(s: str) -> str:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Strip LaTeX formatting to get a plain number.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    s = re.sub(r\\\"\\\\\\\\text\\\\{[^}]*\\\\}\\\", \\\"\\\", s)\\n    s = re.sub(r\\\"\\\\\\\\(mathrm|mathbf|textbf)\\\\{([^}]*)\\\\}\\\", r\\\"\\\\2\\\", s)\\n    s = s.replace(\\\"\\\\\\\\!\\\", \\\"\\\").replace(\\\"\\\\\\\\,\\\", \\\"\\\").replace(\\\"\\\\\\\\;\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n    s = s.replace(\\\"\\\\\\\\$\\\", \\\"\\\").replace(\\\"$\\\", \\\"\\\").replace(\\\"\\\\\\\\%\\\", \\\"\\\").replace(\\\"%\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n    s = re.sub(r\\\"\\\\\\\\[a-zA-Z]+\\\", \\\"\\\", s)\\n    s = s.replace(\\\",\\\", \\\"\\\").replace(\\\" \\\", \\\"\\\").replace(\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n    return s.strip()\\n\\n\\ndef extract_answer(text: str) -> str | None:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract numeric answer from model output.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    clean = re.sub(r\\\"&lt;think>.*?&lt;/think>\\\", \\\"\\\", text, flags=re.DOTALL)\\n    if not clean.strip():\\n        clean = text\\n\\n    boxed = _extract_boxed(clean)\\n    if boxed is not None:\\n        return _clean_latex_number(boxed)\\n\\n    hash_match = re.findall(r\\\"####\\\\s*(.+)\\\", clean)\\n    if hash_match:\\n        return hash_match[-1].strip().replace(\\\",\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n\\n    numbers = re.findall(r\\\"-?\\\\d+(?:,\\\\d{3})*(?:\\\\.\\\\d+)?\\\", clean)\\n    if numbers:\\n        return numbers[-1].replace(\\\",\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n    return None\\n\\n\\ndef normalize_answer(ans: str) -> str | None:\\n    ans = ans.strip().replace(\\\",\\\", \\\"\\\").replace(\\\" \\\", \\\"\\\")\\n    if not ans:\\n        return None\\n    try:\\n        val = float(ans)\\n        if not math.isfinite(val):\\n            return None\\n        if val == int(val):\\n            return str(int(val))\\n        return str(val)\\n    except (ValueError, OverflowError):\\n        return ans\\n\\n\\ndef extract_gold_answer(answer_text: str) -> str:\\n    match = re.search(r\\\"####\\\\s*(.+)\\\", answer_text)\\n    if match:\\n        result = normalize_answer(match.group(1))\\n        return result if result is not None else match.group(1).strip()\\n    return answer_text.strip()\\n\\n\\ndef binomial_stderr(p: float, n: int) -> float:\\n    if n == 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    return math.sqrt(p * (1 - p) / n)\\n\\n\\ndef evaluate(model, tokenizer, dataset, max_new_tokens: int = 4096,\\n             batch_size: int = 1, temperature: float = 0.0, do_sample: bool = False):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Run greedy evaluation and return accuracy + per-example results + EOS stats.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    model.eval()\\n    device = next(model.parameters()).device\\n    eos_token_id = tokenizer.eos_token_id\\n\\n    correct = 0\\n    total = 0\\n    results = []\\n    n_natural_eos = 0\\n    n_truncated = 0\\n    response_lengths = []\\n\\n    gen_kwargs = dict(\\n        max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,\\n        pad_token_id=tokenizer.pad_token_id or tokenizer.eos_token_id,\\n    )\\n    if do_sample:\\n        gen_kwargs[\\\"do_sample\\\"] = True\\n        gen_kwargs[\\\"temperature\\\"] = temperature\\n        gen_kwargs[\\\"top_p\\\"] = 0.95\\n    else:\\n        gen_kwargs[\\\"do_sample\\\"] = False\\n\\n    logger.info(f\\\"Generation \\n\\n...    \\\"num_valid_samples\\\": len(valid_answers),\\n            \\\"answer_distribution\\\": dict(Counter(valid_answers).most_common()) if valid_answers else {},\\n            \\\"samples\\\": sample_details,\\n        })\\n\\n        if total % 50 == 0:\\n            logger.info(\\n                f\\\"Progress: {total}/{len(dataset)}, acc={correct/total:.4f}\\\"\\n            )\\n\\n    accuracy = correct / total if total > 0 else 0.0\\n    stderr = binomial_stderr(accuracy, total)\\n    total_generations = n_natural_eos + n_truncated\\n    eos_stats = {\\n
